0016:             <xd:p><xd:b>Purpose:</xd:b> Provides general purpose display of CDA release 2.0 and 2.1 (Specification: ANSI/HL7 CDAR2) and CDA release 3 (Specification was pulled after ballot) documents. For header attributes it tries to be smart in displaying essentials, which is still a lot.</xd:p>

0205:         <xd:desc>
0206:             <xd:p>Security parameter. When set to 'yes' limits the URIs to images (if any) to locally attached images and/or images that are on the <xd:ref name="external-image-whitelist" type="parameter">external-image-whitelist</xd:ref>. When set to anything other than 'yes' also allows for arbitrary external images (e.g. through http:// or https://). Default value is 'yes' which is considered defensive against potential security risks that could stem from resources loaded from arbitrary source.</xd:p>
0207:         </xd:desc>

0210:     
0211:     <xd:doc>
0212:         <xd:desc>
0213:             <xd:p>Security parameter. When set to 'yes' <xd:a href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/origin.html#sandboxed-plugins-browsing-context-flag">sandboxes the iframe for pdfs</xd:a>. Sandboxed iframe disallow plug-ins, including the plug-in needed to render pdf. Effectively this setting thus prohibits pdf rendering. When set to anything other than 'yes', pdf carrying iframes are not sandboxed and pdf rendering is possible. Default value is 'yes' which is considered defensive against potential security risks that could stem from resources loaded from arbitrary source.</xd:p>
0214:         </xd:desc>

0224:         <xd:desc>Privacy parameter. Accepts a comma separated list of patient ID root values (normally OID's). When a patient ID is encountered with a root value in this list, then the rendering of the extension will be xxx-xxx-xxx regardless of what the actual value is. This is useful to prevent public display of for example the US SSN. Default is to render any ID as it occurs in the document. Note that this setting only affects human rendering and that it does not affect automated processing of the underlying document. If the same value also occurs in the <xd:ref name="skip-ids" type="parameter">skip-ids</xd:ref> list, then that takes precedence.</xd:desc>
0225:     </xd:doc>

Default is to render any ID as it occurs in the document. Note that this setting only affects human rendering and that it does not affect automated processing of the underlying document.</xd:desc>

0758:                     <xsl:comment> Javascript for Revisions switch </xsl:comment>
0759:                     <script type="text/javascript">
0760:                         <xsl:text>var gStringCollapse = "</xsl:text>
0761:                         <xsl:call-template name="getLocalizedString">
0762:                             <xsl:with-param name="key" select="'Collapse'"/>
0763:                         </xsl:call-template>
0764:                         <xsl:text>"; </xsl:text>
0765:                         <xsl:text>var gStringExpand = "</xsl:text>
0766:                         <xsl:call-template name="getLocalizedString">
0767:                             <xsl:with-param name="key" select="'Expand'"/>
0768:                         </xsl:call-template>
0769:                         <xsl:text>";</xsl:text>
0770:                         <xsl:text>
0771:                         function expandAllSections() {
0772:                             var toggleButtons = document.getElementsByClassName("expandCollapse");
0773:                             if (toggleButtons != null) {
0774:                                 var i = 0;
0775:                                 while (i != toggleButtons.length) {
0776:                                     expandSection(toggleButtons[i]);
0777:                                     i++;
0778:                                 }
0779:                             }
0780:                             toggle('sectionsToggleExpand');
0781:                             toggle('sectionsToggleCollapse');
0782:                         }
0783:                         function expandSection(sectionTitleButton) {
0784:                             sectionTitleButton.title = gStringCollapse;
0785:                             sectionTitleButton.innerText = '▼';
0786:                             sectionTitleButton.onclick = function (){collapseSection(this);};
0787:                             var sectionContent = sectionTitleButton.parentElement.parentElement.children[1]
0788:                             if (sectionContent != null) {
0789:                                 sectionContent.style.display = '';
0790:                             }
0791:                         }
0792:                         function collapseAllSections() {
0793:                             var toggleButtons = document.getElementsByClassName("expandCollapse");
0794:                             if (toggleButtons != null) {
0795:                                 var i = 0;
0796:                                 while (i != toggleButtons.length) {
0797:                                     collapseSection(toggleButtons[i]);
0798:                                     i++;
0799:                                 }
0800:                             }
0801:                             toggle('sectionsToggleExpand');
0802:                             toggle('sectionsToggleCollapse');
0803:                         }
0804:                         function collapseSection(sectionTitleButton) {
0805:                             sectionTitleButton.title = gStringExpand;
0806:                             sectionTitleButton.innerText = '▶';
0807:                             sectionTitleButton.onclick = function (){expandSection(this);};
0808:                             var sectionContent = sectionTitleButton.parentElement.parentElement.children[1]
0809:                             if (sectionContent != null) {
0810:                                 sectionContent.style.display = 'none';
0811:                             }
0812:                         }
0813:                         function showReviewMarks() {
0814:                             var allHTMLTags=document.getElementsByTagName("*");
0815:                             for (i in allHTMLTags) {
0816:                                 //Get all tags with the specified class name.
0817:                                 if (allHTMLTags[i].className=='revision_insert_final') {
0818:                                     allHTMLTags[i].className = 'revision_insert';
0819:                                 }
0820:                                 if (allHTMLTags[i].className=='revision_delete_final') {
0821:                                     allHTMLTags[i].className = 'revision_delete';
0822:                                 }
0823:                             }
0824:                             toggle('revisionToggleOn');
0825:                             toggle('revisionToggleOff');
0826:                         }
0827:                         function hideReviewMarks() {
0828:                             var allHTMLTags=document.getElementsByTagName("*");
0829:                             for (i in allHTMLTags) {
0830:                                 //Get all tags with the specified class name.
0831:                                 if (allHTMLTags[i].className=='revision_insert') {
0832:                                     allHTMLTags[i].className = 'revision_insert_final';
0833:                                 }
0834:                                 if (allHTMLTags[i].className=='revision_delete') {
0835:                                     allHTMLTags[i].className = 'revision_delete_final';
0836:                                 }
0837:                             }
0838:                             toggle('revisionToggleOn');
0839:                             toggle('revisionToggleOff');
0840:                         }
0841:                         function toggle(obj) {
0842:                             var el = document.getElementById(obj);
0843:                             el.style.display = (el.style.display != 'none' ? 'none' : '' );
0844:                         }
0845:                         </xsl:text>
0846:                     </script>

0958:     <xd:doc>
0959:         <xd:desc>
0960:             <xd:p>Handle section order by looking at <xd:ref name="section-order" type="parameter"/>. Recursive template</xd:p>
0961:         </xd:desc>
0962:         <xd:param name="thisCode">Current code to take into account</xd:param>
0963:         <xd:param name="residualCodes">Remaining part of the string with (comma separated) codes</xd:param>
0964:     </xd:doc>
0965:     <xsl:template name="handleSectionOrder">

0968:         
0969:         <xsl:for-each select="hl7:component/hl7:section[hl7:code[@code = $thisCode]]">

0972:             </xsl:call-template>

0977:                 <xsl:with-param name="residualCodes" select="substring-after($residualCodes, ',')"/>
0978:             </xsl:call-template>
0979:         </xsl:if>

1817:                 whole document. Also adds a title with the first 50 characters of th footnote on the number so you 
1818:                 don't have to navigate to the footnote and just continue to read.</xd:p>

1826:                     <xsl:value-of select="position()"/>
1827:                 </xsl:if>
1828:             </xsl:for-each>
1829:         </xsl:variable>
1830:         <xsl:variable name="footNoteText">
1831:             <xsl:copy-of select="//hl7:footnote[@ID = $idref]//text()"/>
1832:         </xsl:variable>
1833:         <sup>
1834:             <xsl:text>[</xsl:text>
1835:             <a href="#{$idref}">
1836:                 <!-- Render footnoteref with the first 50 characters of the text -->
1837:                 <xsl:attribute name="title">
1838:                     <xsl:value-of select="substring($footNoteText, 1, 50)"/>
1839:                     <xsl:if test="string-length($footNoteText) > 50">
1840:                         <xsl:text>...</xsl:text>
1841:                     </xsl:if>
1842:                 </xsl:attribute>
1843:                 <xsl:value-of select="$footNoteNum"/>
1844:             </a>
1845:             <xsl:text>]</xsl:text>
1846:         </sup>
1847:     </xsl:template>

2026:     <xsl:template name="check-external-image-whitelist">
2027:         <xsl:param name="current-whitelist"/>
2028:         <xsl:param name="image-uri"/>
2029:         <xsl:param name="altTitleText"/>
2030:         <xsl:choose>
2031:             <xsl:when test="string-length($current-whitelist) &gt; 0">
2032:                 <xsl:variable name="whitelist-item">
2033:                     <xsl:choose>
2034:                         <xsl:when test="contains($current-whitelist,'|')">
2035:                             <xsl:value-of select="substring-before($current-whitelist,'|')"/>
2036:                         </xsl:when>
2037:                         <xsl:otherwise>
2038:                             <xsl:value-of select="$current-whitelist"/>
2039:                         </xsl:otherwise>
2040:                     </xsl:choose>
2041:                 </xsl:variable>
2042:                 <xsl:choose>
2043:                     <xsl:when test="starts-with($image-uri,$whitelist-item)">
2044:                         <br clear="all"/>
2045:                         <img src="{$image-uri}" alt="{$altTitleText}" title="{$altTitleText}"/>
2046:                         <xsl:message>
2047:                             <xsl:value-of select="$image-uri"/>
2048:                             <xsl:call-template name="getLocalizedString">
2049:                                 <xsl:with-param name="key" select="'is-in-the-whitelist'"/>
2050:                             </xsl:call-template>
2051:                         </xsl:message>
2052:                     </xsl:when>
2053:                     <xsl:otherwise>
2054:                         <xsl:call-template name="check-external-image-whitelist">
2055:                             <xsl:with-param name="current-whitelist" select="substring-after($current-whitelist,'|')"/>
2056:                             <xsl:with-param name="image-uri" select="$image-uri"/>
2057:                             <xsl:with-param name="altTitleText" select="$altTitleText"/>
2058:                         </xsl:call-template>
2059:                     </xsl:otherwise>
2060:                 </xsl:choose>
2061:                 
2062:             </xsl:when>
2063:             <xsl:otherwise>

2065:                     <xsl:call-template name="getLocalizedString">
2066:                         <xsl:with-param name="key" select="'non-local-image-found-1'"/>
2067:                     </xsl:call-template>
2068:                     <xsl:value-of select="$image-uri"/>

6442:     <xsl:key name="util-i18nkey" match="translation" use="@key"/>
6443:     
6444:     <xd:doc>
6445:         <xd:desc>
6446:             <xd:p>Retrieves a language dependant string from our <xd:ref name="vocFile" type="parameter">language file</xd:ref> such as a label based on a key. Returns string based on <xd:ref name="textLang" type="parameter">textLang</xd:ref>, <xd:ref name="textLangDefault" type="parameter">textLangDefault</xd:ref>, the first two characters of the textLangDefault, e.g. 'en' in 'en-US' and finally if all else fails just the key text.</xd:p>
6447:         </xd:desc>
6448:         <xd:param name="pre">Some text or space to prefix our string with</xd:param>
6449:         <xd:param name="key">The key to find our text with</xd:param>
6450:         <xd:param name="post">Some text like a colon or space to postfix our text with</xd:param>
6451:     </xd:doc>
6452:     <xsl:template name="getLocalizedString">
6453:         <xsl:param name="pre" select="''"/>
6454:         <xsl:param name="key"/>
6455:         <xsl:param name="post" select="''"/>
6456:         
6457:         <xsl:for-each select="$vocMessages">
6458:             <xsl:variable name="translation" select="key('util-i18nkey', $key)"/>
6459:             <xsl:choose>
6460:                 <!-- compare 'de-CH' -->
6461:                 <xsl:when test="$translation/value[@lang = $textLangLowerCase]">
6462:                     <xsl:value-of select="concat($pre, $translation/value[@lang = $textLangLowerCase]/text(), $post)"/>
6463:                 </xsl:when>
6464:                 <!-- compare 'de' in 'de-CH' -->
6465:                 <xsl:when test="$translation/value[substring(@lang, 1, 2) = $textLangPartLowerCase]">
6466:                     <xsl:value-of select="concat($pre, $translation/value[substring(@lang, 1, 2) = $textLangPartLowerCase]/text(), $post)"/>
6467:                 </xsl:when>
6468:                 <!-- compare 'en-US' -->
6469:                 <xsl:when test="$translation/value[@lang = $textLangDefaultLowerCase]">
6470:                     <xsl:value-of select="concat($pre, $translation/value[@lang = $textLangDefaultLowerCase]/text(), $post)"/>
6471:                 </xsl:when>
6472:                 <!-- compare 'en' in 'en-US' -->
6473:                 <xsl:when test="$translation/value[substring(@lang, 1, 2) = $textLangDefaultPartLowerCase]">
6474:                     <xsl:value-of select="concat($pre, $translation/value[substring(@lang, 1, 2) = $textLangDefaultPartLowerCase]/text(), $post)"/>
6475:                 </xsl:when>
6476:                 <xsl:when test="$translation/value[@lang = 'en-us']">
6477:                     <xsl:value-of select="concat($pre, $translation/text(), $post)"/>
6478:                 </xsl:when>
6479:                 <xsl:otherwise>
6480:                     <xsl:value-of select="concat($pre, $key, $post)"/>
6481:                 </xsl:otherwise>
6482:             </xsl:choose>
6483:         </xsl:for-each>
6484:     </xsl:template>

6559:             <td style="width: 35%; background-color: white;">
6560:                 <!-- produce table of contents -->
6561:                 <ul id="{$tocid}">
6562:                     <li style="list-style: none;">
6563:                         <div class="span_button">
6564:                             <xsl:call-template name="getLocalizedString">
6565:                                 <xsl:with-param name="key" select="'Table of Contents'"/>
6566:                             </xsl:call-template>
6567:                             <xsl:text>&#160;&#8711;</xsl:text>
6568:                         </div>
6569:                         <ul>
6570:                             <xsl:choose>
6571:                                 <xsl:when test="string-length($section-order) = 0">
6572:                                     <xsl:for-each select="$sections">
6573:                                         <xsl:call-template name="make-tableofcontents-item"/>
6574:                                     </xsl:for-each>
6575:                                 </xsl:when>
6576:                                 <xsl:otherwise>
6577:                                     <xsl:call-template name="handleTableOfContentsSectionOrder">
6578:                                         <xsl:with-param name="thisCode" select="substring-before($section-order-var, ',')"/>
6579:                                         <xsl:with-param name="residualCodes" select="substring-after($section-order-var, ',')"/>
6580:                                     </xsl:call-template>
6581:                                     <xsl:for-each select="$sections[hl7:code[not(contains(concat(',', $section-order-var), concat(',', @code, ',')))]]">
6582:                                         <xsl:call-template name="make-tableofcontents-item"/>
6583:                                     </xsl:for-each>
6584:                                 </xsl:otherwise>
6585:                             </xsl:choose>
6586:                         </ul>
6587:                     </li>
6588:                 </ul>
6589:             </td>
6590:         </xsl:if>
6591:     </xsl:template>
6592:     
6593:     <xd:doc>
6594:         <xd:desc>
6595:             <xd:p>Handle section order by looking at <xd:ref name="section-order" type="parameter"/>. Recursive template</xd:p>
6596:         </xd:desc>
6597:         <xd:param name="thisCode">Current code to take into account</xd:param>
6598:         <xd:param name="residualCodes">Remaining part of the string with (comma separated) codes</xd:param>
6599:     </xd:doc>
6600:     <xsl:template name="handleTableOfContentsSectionOrder">
6601:         <xsl:param name="thisCode"/>
6602:         <xsl:param name="residualCodes"/>
6603:         
6604:         <xsl:for-each select="hl7:component/hl7:structuredBody/hl7:component/hl7:section[hl7:code[@code = $thisCode]]">
6605:             <xsl:call-template name="make-tableofcontents-item"/>
6606:         </xsl:for-each>
6607:         <xsl:if test="string-length($residualCodes) &gt; 0">
6608:             <xsl:call-template name="handleTableOfContentsSectionOrder">
6609:                 <xsl:with-param name="thisCode" select="substring-before($residualCodes, ',')"/>
6610:                 <xsl:with-param name="residualCodes" select="substring-after($residualCodes, ',')"/>
6611:             </xsl:call-template>
6612:         </xsl:if>
6613:     </xsl:template>
6614:     
6615:     <xd:doc>
6616:         <xd:desc>Create the contents of a TOC item</xd:desc>
6617:     </xd:doc>
6618:     <xsl:template name="make-tableofcontents-item">
6619:         <li>
6620:             <a>
6621:                 <xsl:attribute name="href">
6622:                     <xsl:text>#</xsl:text>
6623:                     <xsl:choose>
6624:                         <xsl:when test="@ID">
6625:                             <xsl:value-of select="@ID"/>
6626:                         </xsl:when>
6627:                         <xsl:otherwise>
6628:                             <xsl:apply-templates select="." mode="getAnchorName"/>
6629:                         </xsl:otherwise>
6630:                     </xsl:choose>
6631:                 </xsl:attribute>
6632:                 <xsl:apply-templates select="." mode="getTitleName"/>
6633:                 <xsl:if test="@nullFlavor">
6634:                     <xsl:text> (</xsl:text>
6635:                     <xsl:call-template name="show-nullFlavor">
6636:                         <xsl:with-param name="in" select="@nullFlavor"/>
6637:                     </xsl:call-template>
6638:                     <xsl:text>)</xsl:text>
6639:                 </xsl:if>
6640:                 <xsl:if test="$menu-depth > 1 and hl7:component/hl7:section">
6641:                     <xsl:text> ▶</xsl:text>
6642:                 </xsl:if>
6643:             </a>
6644:             <xsl:if test="$menu-depth > 1 and hl7:component/hl7:section">
6645:                 <ul>
6646:                     <xsl:for-each select="hl7:component/hl7:section">
6647:                         <li style="padding-left: 2em;">
6648:                             <a>
6649:                                 <xsl:attribute name="href">
6650:                                     <xsl:text>#</xsl:text>
6651:                                     <xsl:choose>
6652:                                         <xsl:when test="@ID">
6653:                                             <xsl:value-of select="@ID"/>
6654:                                         </xsl:when>
6655:                                         <xsl:otherwise>
6656:                                             <xsl:apply-templates select="." mode="getAnchorName"/>
6657:                                         </xsl:otherwise>
6658:                                     </xsl:choose>
6659:                                 </xsl:attribute>
6660:                                 <xsl:apply-templates select="." mode="getTitleName"/>
6661:                                 <xsl:if test="$menu-depth > 2 and hl7:component/hl7:section">
6662:                                     <xsl:text> ▶</xsl:text>
6663:                                 </xsl:if>
6664:                             </a>
6665:                             <xsl:if test="$menu-depth > 2 and hl7:component/hl7:section">
6666:                                 <ul>
6667:                                     <xsl:for-each select="hl7:component/hl7:section">
6668:                                         <li style="padding-left: 2em;">
6669:                                             <a>
6670:                                                 <xsl:attribute name="href">
6671:                                                     <xsl:text>#</xsl:text>
6672:                                                     <xsl:choose>
6673:                                                         <xsl:when test="@ID">
6674:                                                             <xsl:value-of select="@ID"/>
6675:                                                         </xsl:when>
6676:                                                         <xsl:otherwise>
6677:                                                             <xsl:apply-templates select="." mode="getAnchorName"/>
6678:                                                         </xsl:otherwise>
6679:                                                     </xsl:choose>
6680:                                                 </xsl:attribute>
6681:                                                 <xsl:apply-templates select="." mode="getTitleName"/>
6682:                                             </a>
6683:                                         </li>
6684:                                     </xsl:for-each>
6685:                                 </ul>
6686:                             </xsl:if>
6687:                         </li>
6688:                     </xsl:for-each>
6689:                 </ul>
6690:             </xsl:if>
6691:         </li>
6692:     </xsl:template>
